Triggers: sys-doctor, system health, journal errors, failed units, lockup, hard reset, last boot, coredump, thermal, battery drain, resume problems, "what's wrong with this machine". ([AndrewCull/nixos-config](https://github.com/AndrewCull/nixos-config/tree/HEAD/agents/skills/nixos-doctor) · ★1)
- **nixos-dotnet-env-config**: Configure .NET NixOS services via env vars. ([grapefruit89/mediNix-core](https://github.com/grapefruit89/mediNix-core/tree/HEAD/docs/agents/07-patterns-misc/nixos-dotnet-env-config))
- **nixos-dry-run**: This skill should be used when the user wants to "dry run", "preview rebuild", "see what would change", "check config", "test build", or "see changes before applying". Use it to preview what nh os boot would change without writing anything to the system. ([CommanderBosko/NixOS](https://github.com/CommanderBosko/NixOS/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/nixos-dry-run))
